1,302 entries, review-branch to roleplay-shinycolors. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **review-branch**: Self-reviews the current branch before a PR (superseded by built-in /code-review). ([houshuang/awesome-claude-code](https://github.com/houshuang/awesome-claude-code/tree/HEAD/commands/review-branch) · ★3)
- **review-branch**: Deep multi-lens review of an MR, PR, or any local branch. Dispatches 4 parallel subagents (architecture, security, test coverage, naming/API) inside an isolated worktree, writes structured findings to a central review store, and serves a collaborative HTML tracker from a local daemon with a per-finding post toggle, editable comment drafts, and notes to Claude. Nothing auto-posts; posting happens through companion comment skills only when explicitly asked. Works against GitLab (glab) and GitHub (gh); auto-detects from the git remote. ([tednaleid/claude-plugins](https://github.com/tednaleid/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/review-branch) · ★2 · 3 skills, 4 agents)
